selection:text-on-primary-fixed"><header class="fixed top-0 w-full z-50 pt-safe bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]"><div class="h-16 px-margin-mobile flex items-center justify-between"><div class="flex items-center gap-unit-3"><div class="flex flex-col"><div class="flex items-center gap-unit-1"><span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-primary font-medium tracking-tight">WhiteBoard</span></div><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant font-medium tracking-wider uppercase opacity-80">Ask. Learn. Become.</span></div></div><div class="flex items-center gap-unit-2"><button aria-label="Search topics" class="w-11 h-11 flex items-center justify-center text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface rounded-full transition-colors" type="button"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">search</span></button><a aria-label="Open profile" class="w-11 h-11 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full" data-path="me" href="#"><div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-primary flex items-center justify-center"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-on-primary text-[18px]">person</span></div></a></div></div></header><main class="flex-1 flex flex-col relative w-full pt-16 pb-24 bg-surface px-margin-mobile"><div class="flex flex-col w-full gap-unit-6 pt-unit-2">
<!-- Greeting Header Section -->
<section class="flex flex-col gap-unit-1">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-unit-2 text-tertiary-container">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]" style="font-variation-settings: &quot;FILL&quot; 1;">wb_sunny</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm tracking-normal font-medium text-secondary">Thursday Study Desk</span>
</div>
<span class="font-code-sm text-code-sm text-on-surface-variant/70">Grade 8 CBC</span>
</div>
<div class="mt-unit-1">
<h1 class="font-display-md-mobile text-display-md-mobile text-primary tracking-tight font-normal">
        Good morning, Amina
      </h1>
<p class="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant mt-0.5">
        What will you learn today?
      </p>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Streak & Momentum Indicator Card -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-unit-4 shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(28,32,51,0.04)] flex items-center gap-unit-3">
<div class="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-tertiary-fixed flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-tertiary-container text-[20px]" style="font-variation-settings: &quot;FILL&quot; 1;">local_fire_department</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col min-w-0 flex-1">
<div class="flex items-center gap-unit-1">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface">3-day learning streak</span>
<span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-tertiary-container"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-tertiary-container font-medium">Active</span>
</div>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant truncate">
        15 mins daily goal reached yesterday
      </p>
</div>
<div class="shrink-0 flex -space-x-1">
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-tertiary-container"></span>
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-tertiary-container"></span>
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-tertiary-container"></span>
<span class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full bg-secondary-container"></span>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Live Upcoming Class Banner (High Priority Focus) -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-unit-6 shadow-[0_8px_20px_-6px_rgba(28,32,51,0.06)] relative overflow-hidden">
<div class="absolute top-0 left-0 w-1.5 h-full bg-primary-container"></div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-unit-3">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-unit-2">
<span class="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-tertiary-container animate-pulse"></span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-tertiary-container font-semibold">Live in 35 mins</span>
</div>
<span class="font-code-sm text-code-sm text-on-surface-variant bg-surface-container px-unit-2 py-0.5 rounded">4:00 PM</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-unit-1">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary">Next Class with Teacher Sarah</span>
<h2 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface font-medium leading-snug">
          Quadratic Equations &amp; Graphing
        </h2>
</div>
<div class="flex items-center gap-unit-3 pt-unit-1">
<div class="relative w-9 h-9 rounded-full overflow-hidden shrink-0 bg-surface-variant">
<img class="w-full h-full object-cover" data-alt="Close up warm academic portrait of Teacher Sarah, an engaging instructor with soft rim light and warm neutral background" src="https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/aida-public/AB6AXuBBeJkMSWhk1Aa3LHkUswZzCo2W7FfvD86KgcJbFNfNtqXoVrWtGULB0ztIRzUm0ZTTwSHhf3SjWAmO3Wty4bmmmheKlKZTi8IZfBAzXz_RUmtj-9GQXAUEl44WpVMZZMUkBhevrchqqk3SE04uNRvVUG97RFbwJAnz6lajlz85HKxkFqhYxGjSEGccBU1xLUm8j3sSHCHb8ozZDfsbWm_cNC73eiVnahJN_LyJLOvlwTymVg9sRGvO">
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface">Teacher Sarah Mutua</span>
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">Interactive Studio 3</span>
</div>
</div>
<div class="pt-unit-2 flex items-center gap-unit-3">
<a class="flex-1 bg-primary-container hover:bg-primary text-on-primary font-label-md text-label-md py-unit-3 px-unit-4 rounded flex items-center justify-center gap-unit-2 transition-transform active:scale-[0.99] shadow-sm" href="#">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">meeting_room</span>
<span class="">Join Session</span>
</a>
<button aria-label="Add to notebook calendar" class="w-11 h-11 rounded bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container flex items-center justify-center hover:bg-surface-variant transition-colors" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">bookmark_add</span>
</button>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Subject Mastery Tracking -->
<section class="flex flex-col gap-unit-3">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between px-unit-1">
<div class="flex items-center gap-unit-2">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-primary text-[20px]">draw</span>
<h3 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-primary">Curriculum Mastery</h3>
</div>
<a class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary-container hover:text-primary transition-colors flex items-center" href="#">
        All subjects
        <span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[16px]">arrow_forward</span>
</a>
</div>
<!-- Subject Cards Deck -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-unit-3">
<!-- Subject 1: Mathematics -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-unit-4 shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(28,32,51,0.03)] flex flex-col gap-unit-3">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary">CBC Core · Formative Units</span>
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">Mathematics</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-tertiary-fixed-variant bg-surface-container-high px-unit-2 py-0.5 rounded">
            82%
          </span>
</div>
<!-- Custom Tactile Progress Rule -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<div class="w-full bg-surface-container h-2 rounded overflow-hidden">
<div class="bg-primary-container h-full rounded transition-all duration-500" style="width: 82%;"></div>
</div>
<div class="flex justify-between items-center text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm">4 of 5 units completed</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary">Mastering graphs</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Subject 2: English Language & Literature -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-unit-4 shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(28,32,51,0.03)] flex flex-col gap-unit-3">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary">Language Arts</span>
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">English Language &amp; Literature</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-on-surface-variant bg-surface-container px-unit-2 py-0.5 rounded">
            58%
          </span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<div class="w-full bg-surface-container h-2 rounded overflow-hidden">
<div class="bg-primary h-full rounded transition-all duration-500" style="width: 58%;"></div>
</div>
<div class="flex justify-between items-center text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm">Narrative analysis focus</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary">3 units open</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<!-- Subject 3: Integrated Science -->
<div class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-unit-4 shadow-[0_2px_6px_rgba(28,32,51,0.03)] flex flex-col gap-unit-3">
<div class="flex items-start justify-between">
<div class="flex flex-col">
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary">Natural Sciences</span>
<span class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">Integrated Science</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-md text-label-md text-primary bg-primary-fixed px-unit-2 py-0.5 rounded">
            74%
          </span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
<div class="w-full bg-surface-container h-2 rounded overflow-hidden">
<div class="bg-primary-container h-full rounded transition-all duration-500" style="width: 74%;"></div>
</div>
<div class="flex justify-between items-center text-on-surface-variant">
<span class="font-body-sm text-body-sm">Cellular biology lab ready</span>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-primary">On schedule</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Practice Quiz Ready Card -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-low rounded-xl p-unit-4 shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(28,32,51,0.03)] flex flex-col gap-unit-3">
<div class="flex items-center justify-between">
<div class="flex items-center gap-unit-2">
<div class="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-secondary-container flex items-center justify-center text-primary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">quiz</span>
</div>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-secondary">Quick Check</span>
</div>
<span class="font-code-sm text-code-sm text-on-surface-variant">5 questions</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-unit-1">
<h4 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">
        Algebraic Expressions Quiz Ready
      </h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
        Curated from your last margin notes. Solve simple factoring and like-terms.
      </p>
</div>
<div class="pt-unit-1">
<a class="inline-flex items-center gap-unit-2 text-primary font-label-md text-label-md hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" href="#">
<span class="">Start Quiz</span>
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[18px]">arrow_forward</span>
</a>
</div>
</section>
<!-- Physical Whiteboard / Worksheet Upload Invite (Empty/Action State) -->
<section class="bg-surface-container-lowest rounded-xl p-unit-6 shadow-[0_2px_10px_rgba(28,32,51,0.03)] flex flex-col gap-unit-4 text-center items-center">
<div class="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-surface-container flex items-center justify-center text-primary">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[28px]">document_scanner</span>
</div>
<div class="flex flex-col gap-unit-1 max-w-[280px]">
<h4 class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm text-on-surface">
        Need practice on another topic?
      </h4>
<p class="font-body-sm text-body-sm text-on-surface-variant">
        Upload any textbook page or handwritten worksheet. WhiteBoard will generate interactive exercises to practice with.
      </p>
</div>
<button class="w-full bg-surface-container hover:bg-surface-container-high text-primary font-label-md text-label-md py-unit-3 px-unit-4 rounded flex items-center justify-center gap-unit-2 transition-colors active:scale-[0.99]" type="button">
<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">upload_file</span>
<span class="">Upload Worksheet</span>
</button>
</section>
<!-- Editorial Marginalia Notebook Quote -->
<footer class="pt-unit-2 pb-unit-4 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center gap-unit-1">
<p class="font-headline-sm text-headline-sm italic text-secondary text-[1.1rem]">
      “Ask. Learn. Become.”
    </p>
<span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm text-on-surface-variant/60">
      Academic Workspace · WhiteBoard
    </span>
</footer>
</div></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/80 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-1px_8px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-semibold"><div class="h-16 px-unit-2 flex items-center justify-around"><a aria-current="page" class="flex-1 min-h-[44px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 transition-colors text-primary font-semibold" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">stylus_note</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Home</span></a><a class="flex-1 min-h-[44px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="learn" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">auto_stories</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Learn</span></a><a class="flex-1 min-h-[44px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="classes" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">meeting_room</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Classes</span></a><a class="flex-1 min-h-[44px] flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors" data-path="me" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">account_circle</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Me</span></a></div></nav>

</body></html>

<script>
  // Bookmark toggle micro-interaction
  document.querySelectorAll('.bookmark-toggle').forEach(button => {
    button.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      const icon = button.querySelector('.material-symbols-outlined');
      if (icon.textContent === 'bookmark_border') {
        icon.textContent = 'bookmark';
        icon.style.fontVariationSettings = "'FILL' 1";
        button.classList.add('text-primary');
      } else {
        icon.textContent = 'bookmark_border';
        icon.style.fontVariationSettings = "'FILL' 0";
        button.classList.remove('text-primary');
      }
    });
  });

  // Client-side category filtering ribbon
  const filterButtons = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-chip');
  const cards = document.querySelectorAll('#cardsFeed article');

  filterButtons.forEach(btn => {
    btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
      // Toggle active states
      filterButtons.forEach(b => {
        b.className = "filter-chip whitespace-nowrap px-unit-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-surface-container-lowest text-on-surface font-label-sm text-label-sm shadow-sm hover:bg-surface-container-low transition-all";
      });
      btn.className = "filter-chip whitespace-nowrap px-unit-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container text-on-primary font-label-sm text-label-sm shadow-sm transition-all";

      const tag = btn.getAttribute('data-tag');
      cards.forEach(card => {
        if (tag === 'all') {
          card.classList.remove('hidden');
        } else {
          const categories = card.getAttribute('data-category') || '';
          if (categories.includes(tag)) {
            card.classList.remove('hidden');
          } else {
            card.classList.add('hidden');
          }
        }
      });
    });
  });
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/90 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-2px_12px_rgba(22,26,45,0.04)]" data-active-classes="text-primary-container font-semibold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-16 px-unit-2"><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="home" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">dashboard</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm leading-none">Home</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="learn" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">auto_stories</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm leading-none">Learn</span></a><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 min-w-[56px] h-12 transition-colors text-primary-container font-semibold" data-path="scholarships" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">workspace_premium</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm leading-none">Scholarships</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="marginalia" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">edit_note</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm leading-none">Scratchpad</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 min-w-[56px] h-12 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="profile" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">account_circle</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm leading-none">Profile</span></a></div></nav>

<script>
  // Simple zoom controls for document canvas inspect
  (function() {
    let currentScale = 1;
    const canvas = document.getElementById('certificate-canvas');
    const zoomInBtn = document.getElementById('zoom-in-btn');
    const zoomOutBtn = document.getElementById('zoom-out-btn');
    const zoomLabel = document.getElementById('zoom-label');
    const exportBtn = document.getElementById('export-json-btn');

    function updateZoom(newScale) {
      currentScale = Math.min(Math.max(newScale, 0.75), 1.35);
      if (canvas) {
        canvas.style.transform = `scale(${currentScale})`;
        canvas.style.transformOrigin = 'top center';
      }
      if (zoomLabel) {
        zoomLabel.textContent = `${Math.round(currentScale * 100)}%`;
      }
    }

    if (zoomInBtn) {
      zoomInBtn.addEventListener('click', () => updateZoom(currentScale + 0.1));
    }
    if (zoomOutBtn) {
      zoomOutBtn.addEventListener('click', () => updateZoom(currentScale - 0.1));
    }

    if (exportBtn) {
      exportBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const auditPayload = {
          certificateId: "#WB-2025-EK-0982-CERT",
          candidate: {
            name: "Kwame Mutua",
            id: "WB-9014",
            institution: "Riverside Academy (Center EA-089)",
            cohort: "Year 10 Cambridge IGCSE"
          },
          guardianSignature: {
            name: "Zainab Mutua",
            authChannel: "Safaricom 2FA OTP",
            timestamp: "2025-07-01T14:22:18+03:00"
          },
          ledgerConsensus: {
            network: "East Africa Academic Ledger Network",
            blockNumber: 4829102,
            merkleRoot: "85fa029a1e7488e92bc0d8327a195e2bfd007a4192d6e3c21",
            endorser: "Dr. Angela Kiprop, Ph.D. (Riverside Academy)",
            signature: "0x9482fe01b6e7b1a209ec3849bcde7103"
          }
        };

        const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(auditPayload, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.href = url;
        a.download = "WB-2025-EK-0982-CERT-Audit.json";
        a.click();
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main></div></body></html>

<script>
  (function() {
    const consentToggle = document.getElementById('parent-consent-toggle');
    const dispatchBtn = document.getElementById('dispatch-dossier-btn');

    if (consentToggle && dispatchBtn) {
      consentToggle.addEventListener('change', function() {
        if (this.checked) {
          dispatchBtn.removeAttribute('disabled');
          dispatchBtn.classList.add('animate-bounce');
          setTimeout(() => dispatchBtn.classList.remove('animate-bounce'), 800);
        } else {
          dispatchBtn.setAttribute('disabled', 'true');
        }
      });

      dispatchBtn.addEventListener('click', function() {
        if (!dispatchBtn.hasAttribute('disabled')) {
          const originalText = dispatchBtn.innerHTML;
          dispatchBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px] animate-spin">sync</span><span>Transmitting Cryptographic Dossier...</span>';
          setTimeout(() => {
            dispatchBtn.innerHTML = '<span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[20px]">check_circle</span><span>Dossier Dispatched to Selection Board!</span>';
            dispatchBtn.classList.remove('bg-primary');
            dispatchBtn.classList.add('bg-surface-tint');
          }, 1200);
        }
      });
    }
  })();
</script></main><nav class="fixed bottom-0 w-full z-50 pb-safe bg-surface/85 backdrop-blur-xl shadow-[0_-1px_12px_rgba(0,0,0,0.04)]" data-active-classes="text-primary font-semibold"><div class="flex justify-around items-center h-16 px-gutter-mobile"><a aria-current="page" class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 w-16 h-12 transition-colors text-primary font-semibold" data-path="dossier-overview" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">article</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Dossier</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 w-16 h-12 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="portfolio-artifacts" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">folder_supervised</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Artifacts</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 w-16 h-12 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="telemetry-progress" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">insights</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Telemetry</span></a><a class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-unit-1 w-16 h-12 text-on-surface-variant hover:text-on-surface transition-colors" data-path="academic-endorsements" href="#"><span class="material-symbols-outlined text-[22px]">verified</span><span class="font-label-sm text-label-sm">Endorse</span></a></div></nav></body></html>
